PRODUCTION_AUTH_MAPPING_CONTRACT_DESIGNED_NO_WRITE

Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-AC - Controlled Activity Intake Production Auth Mapping Contract Verification

Purpose
This document defines the production auth mapping contract for Controlled Activity Intake before any production write route or Supabase insert is allowed.

Current safe state
- O-Y-FIX2 integrated authContext.ts into the no-write preview route.
- O-Z independently verified the route/auth integration.
- O-AA allowed production auth mapping contract design only.
- The route remains no_write_preview only.
- The route still requires x-controlled-intake-no-write-preview.
- The route still uses preview app user header only in preview mode.
- No production Auth0/session integration exists in controlled-intake route yet.
- No Supabase insert is approved.
- No database write is approved.
- No AI/category/state processing is approved.

Core production contract
A future production trusted context must be produced only from server-side evidence.

Production trusted context = {
  appUserId: stable application user id resolved from authenticated server identity,
  actorId: optional server-verified actor id,
  organizationId: optional server-verified organization id,
  spaceId: optional server-verified space id,
  requestSource: production route/helper source marker
}

Allowed identity input
- Authenticated server session.
- Auth0/server-side identity subject.
- Server-controlled session metadata, if already available in the project.
- Server-side user sync or profile mapping evidence.

Forbidden identity input
- request body user_id.
- request body appUserId.
- request body actorId.
- request body organizationId.
- request body spaceId.
- client-provided ownership identifiers.
- preview headers in production write mode.

Mapping rule 1 — authenticated identity
The production resolver must first read authenticated identity from server-side auth/session context.
If session identity is missing, it must fail closed.

Expected failure:
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_IDENTITY_REQUIRED

Mapping rule 2 — auth subject
The production resolver must extract a stable auth subject from the server identity.
If subject/sub is missing, blank, or unusable, it must fail closed.

Expected failure:
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED

Mapping rule 3 — app user id
The production resolver must resolve stable appUserId from server-side mapping.
It may use existing project user-sync/profile logic, but O-AB does not select implementation code yet.
If appUserId mapping is missing, it must fail closed.

Expected failure:
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_APP_USER_MAPPING_REQUIRED

Mapping rule 4 — actor / organization / space context
actorId, organizationId and spaceId are optional for the current intake contract.
They may be added only if they are resolved from server-verified context.
They must not be accepted from request body.
If future UI lets the user select organization/space context, the selected value must be checked server-side before becoming trusted context.

Expected future failures:
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_CONTEXT_NOT_VERIFIED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_ORGANIZATION_NOT_ALLOWED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SPACE_NOT_ALLOWED

Mapping rule 5 — preview mode separation
No-write preview mode remains separate from production mode.
Preview headers may continue to exist only for explicitly marked no-write verification paths.
previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite must remain false.

Mapping rule 6 — database boundary
O-AB does not approve database reads or writes.
A later step may design a no-write resolver contract that describes reads, but actual Supabase reads/writes require a separate explicit gate.
Supabase insert into activity_events is still forbidden.

Mapping rule 7 — semantic boundary
Production auth mapping must not trigger semantic processing.
It must not call AI.
It must not run Category Derivation.
It must not persist category candidates.
It must not create Value Objects.
It must not write state facts, deltas, or snapshots.

Recommended future helper boundary
Future helper should be separate from route.ts.

Potential future file:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/productionAuthMapping.ts

Potential future exports:
- CONTROLLED_ACTIVITY_INTAKE_PRODUCTION_AUTH_MAPPING_LAYER
- ControlledActivityIntakeProductionAuthIdentity
- ControlledActivityIntakeProductionAuthMappingInput
- ControlledActivityIntakeProductionAuthMappingResult
- ControlledActivityIntakeProductionAuthMappingFailure
- buildControlledActivityIntakeProductionTrustedContext
- assertControlledActivityIntakeProductionTrustedContext

Recommended future route boundary
route.ts should not directly contain complex mapping logic.
route.ts should:
1. determine mode,
2. reject production mode until approved,
3. delegate trusted context building to a helper,
4. validate body,
5. build payload,
6. build idempotency,
7. build preview/static row or later write row only after explicit write gate.

Contract result shape
Production auth mapping result should follow the existing authContext.ts style:

Success:
- ok: true
- layer
- source: "production_auth_session"
- authSubject
- trustedContext
- guardrails

Failure:
- ok: false
- layer
- source
- code
- message
- guardrails

Required guardrails
- productionAuthMappingOnly: true
- serverSideContextRequired: true
- clientOwnershipAccepted: false
- previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite: false
- dbWriteExecuted: false
- sqlExecuted: false
- aiCallExecuted: false
- semanticCandidatesPersisted: false
- valueObjectsCreated: false
- stateFactsCreated: false
- stateDeltasCreated: false
- stateSnapshotsCreated: false

Open decisions for later steps
1. Exact server API for reading Auth0/session identity.
2. Exact table/helper/source for mapping auth subject to appUserId.
3. Whether appUserId equals current internal user_id or requires translation.
4. How actorId should be resolved for personal/family/business spaces.
5. How organizationId/spaceId should be selected and verified.
6. Whether production controlled-intake route should initially support only personal/self actor context.
7. Whether production route should remain no-write while mapping is integrated.
8. When Supabase reads become allowed.
9. When Supabase insert into activity_events becomes allowed.

O-AB implementation boundary
O-AB creates only this contract document.
O-AB does not modify route.ts.
O-AB does not modify authContext.ts.
O-AB does not create productionAuthMapping.ts.
O-AB does not import Auth0.
O-AB does not import Supabase.
O-AB does not execute database reads or writes.

- Focused auth evidence count: 380
- Focused mapping evidence count: 520
